--- RAG-CDB ---
What are the five commonly taken specimens for patients who received extirpation of matter from left lower lung lobe, via natural or artificial opening endoscopic previously during the same month since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.spec_type_desc FROM ( SELECT T2.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'extirpation of matter from left lower lung lobe, via natural or artificial opening endoscopic' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, microbiologyevents.charttime FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',microbiologyevents.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.spec_type_desc ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.spec_type_desc', 't3.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'microbiologyevents', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'extirpation of matter from left lower lung lobe, via natural or artificial opening endoscopic'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',microbiologyevents.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------

Count the number of unique medicines that patient 10026354 was prescribed in 11/this year.
----------
PREDS:

Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10026354'], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year')"], ["strftime('%m',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '11']]}
----------
TRUTH:

How many patients in 2100 received central venous catheter placement with guidance after the reopening of recent thoracotomy site procedure within the same month?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'central venous catheter placement with guidance' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'central venous catheter placement with guidance'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

In 2100, how many patients received pantoprazole in the same hospital encounter after being diagnosed with defibrination syndrome?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT prescriptions.subject_id) 
FROM prescriptions 
JOIN diagnoses_icd ON prescriptions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id 
WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'pantoprazole' 
AND diagnoses_icd.icd_code = 'icd9|2866' 
AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = '2100'
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['prescriptions.subject_id'], 'tab': ['diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['diagnoses_icd.icd_code', '=', 'icd9|2866'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'pantoprazole'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'defibrination syndrome' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'pantoprazole' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'defibrination syndrome'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'pantoprazole'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id']]}

----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc = 'blood (toxo)' AND microbiologyevents.charttime >= datetime('now', '-1 year')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['microbiologyevents.charttime', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', '=', 'blood (toxo)']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT admissions.subject_id ) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT microbiologyevents.hadm_id FROM microbiologyevents WHERE microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc = 'blood (toxo)' AND datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'microbiologyevents.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', '=', 'blood (toxo)']]}

List the top five most frequent procedures that patients underwent within the same month after being diagnosed with diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND strftime('%m',T1.charttime) = strftime('%m',T2.charttime) GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled'], ["strftime('%m',t1.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%m',t2.charttime)"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

How many distinct medications were ordered for patient 10021118 since 10/2100?

Among patients who were diagnosed with acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous during this year, what are the top five most commonly prescribed medications that followed in the same hospital encounter for patients in their 50s?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
        AND admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What are the three most commonly taken specimens that patients were tested during the same month after receiving a extirpation of matter from left femoral vein, percutaneous approach procedure throughout this year?
----------

What are the four most commonly prescribed drugs for patients who were diagnosed with urinary incontinence, unspecified previously within the same month?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'urinary incontinence, unspecified'
        )
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'urinary incontinence, unspecified'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'urinary incontinence, unspecified' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'urinary incontinence, unspecified'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

What is the 6-month survival probability for patients living with a other and unspecified angina pectoris diagnosis?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified angina pectoris' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/2 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

This year, what are the top five most frequently prescribed medications for patients who were also prescribed alteplase at the same time?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T1.drug FROM (
    SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM prescriptions
    WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    AND prescriptions.subject_id IN (
        SELECT subject_id FROM prescriptions AS p2
        WHERE p2.drug = 'alteplase'
        AND datetime(p2.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    )
    GROUP BY prescriptions.drug
) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [["datetime(p2.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-0 year')"], ['p2.drug', '=', 'alteplase'], ['t1.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'alteplase' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['datetime(t1.starttime)', '=', 'datetime(t2.starttime)'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'alteplase'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

PREDS:

What are the three most frequently ordered medications that female patients aged 60 or above were prescribed within 2 months after they were diagnosed with unspecified essential hypertension in 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id
        WHERE admissions.age >= 60
        AND patients.gender = 'f'
        AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = '2100'
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.age', '>=', '60'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:

    AND prescriptions.hadm_id IN (
        SELECT hadm_id FROM prescriptions WHERE drug = 'fentanyl citrate'

    ) 
    GROUP BY prescriptions.drug 
) AS T1 
WHERE T1.C1 <= 5

What are the five most common microbiology tests for patients that had previously received introduction of other thrombolytic into peripheral vein, percutaneous approach within the same hospital visit since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.test_name FROM (
    SELECT T2.test_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime
        FROM procedures_icd
        JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_procedures
            WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'introduction of other thrombolytic into peripheral vein, percutaneous approach'
        ) AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100'
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.test_name, microbiologyevents.charttime
        FROM microbiologyevents
        JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE strftime('%Y', microbiologyevents.charttime) = '2100'
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    GROUP BY T2.test_name
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.test_name', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.test_name', 't3.test_name'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'microbiologyevents', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'introduction of other thrombolytic into peripheral vein, percutaneous approach'], ["strftime('%y',microbiologyevents.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.test_name FROM ( SELECT T2.test_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'introduction of other thrombolytic into peripheral vein, percutaneous approach' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.test_name, microbiologyevents.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',microbiologyevents.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.test_name ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.test_name', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.test_name', 't3.test_name'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'microbiologyevents', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'introduction of other thrombolytic into peripheral vein, percutaneous approach'], ["strftime('%y',microbiologyevents.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What is the 3-month survival probability for patients living with a asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status diagnosis?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}
----------

What are the three most commonly prescribed drugs for patients who were diagnosed with personal history of tobacco use previously in the same month, since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'personal history of tobacco use'
        )
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'personal history of tobacco use'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

What are the four most frequent drugs that were prescribed to female patients in their 50s during the same hospital encounter after they were diagnosed with unspecified essential hypertension in this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
        AND admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
        AND patients.gender = 'f'
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
        AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

What proportion of patients with a diagnosis of spinal stenosis, lumbar region, with neurogenic claudication survive over a 6-month period?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'spinal stenosis, lumbar region, with neurogenic claudication' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'spinal stenosis, lumbar region, with neurogenic claudication'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/2 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

Among the patients who were prescribed alprazolam, what are the five most frequently prescribed drugs at the same time in this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T1.drug FROM (
    SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM prescriptions
    WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    AND prescriptions.subject_id IN (
        SELECT DISTINCT subject_id
        FROM prescriptions
        WHERE drug = 'alprazolam'
        AND datetime(starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    )
    GROUP BY prescriptions.drug
) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-0 year')"], ["datetime(starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-0 year')"], ['t1.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'alprazolam' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['datetime(t1.starttime)', '=', 'datetime(t2.starttime)'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'alprazolam'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

How many patients received a closed [endoscopic] biopsy of bronchus after the procedure of arterial catheterization during the same hospital encounter?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT procedures_icd.hadm_id) 
FROM procedures_icd 
JOIN procedures_icd AS proc1 ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = proc1.hadm_id 
WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'closed [endoscopic] biopsy of bronchus') 
AND proc1.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'arterial catheterization') 
AND procedures_icd.charttime > proc1.charttime
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['d_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['d_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'arterial catheterization'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'closed [endoscopic] biopsy of bronchus'], ['procedures_icd.charttime', '>', 'proc1.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'arterial catheterization' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'closed [endoscopic] biopsy of bronchus' ) ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'arterial catheterization'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'closed [endoscopic] biopsy of bronchus'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id']]}

For patients who had hemodialysis, what were the most frequent four microbiology tests carried out during the same hospital visit?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.test_name FROM (
    SELECT T2.test_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM procedures_icd
        JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_procedures
            WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'hemodialysis'
        )
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.test_name, microbiologyevents.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM microbiologyevents
        JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime
    GROUP BY T2.test_name
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.test_name', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.test_name', 't3.test_name'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'microbiologyevents', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'hemodialysis'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.test_name FROM ( SELECT T2.test_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'hemodialysis' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.test_name, microbiologyevents.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.test_name ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.test_name', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.test_name', 't3.test_name'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'microbiologyevents', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'hemodialysis'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

What proportion of patients with a diagnosis of acetonuria survive over a 6-month period?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acetonuria' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acetonuria'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/2 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
JOIN procedures_icd AS proc1 ON admissions.hadm_id = proc1.hadm_id 
JOIN procedures_icd AS proc2 ON admissions.hadm_id = proc2.hadm_id 
WHERE proc1.icd_code = (SELECT icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE long_title = 'single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass') 
AND proc2.icd_code = (SELECT icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE long_title = 'coronary arteriography using two catheters') 
AND strftime('%Y', proc1.charttime) = '2100' 
AND strftime('%Y', proc2.charttime) = '2100' 
AND proc1.charttime < proc2.charttime
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['proc1.charttime', '<', 'proc2.charttime'], ["strftime('%y',proc1.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',proc2.charttime)", '=', '2100']]}

PREDS:

Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'injury to thoracic aorta' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other specified forms of effusion, except tuberculous' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'injury to thoracic aorta'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other specified forms of effusion, except tuberculous'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id']]}

Count the number of patients who had arterial catheterization in the same month after having been diagnosed with osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region and thigh in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region and thigh'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'arterial catheterization'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE strftime('%m', T1.charttime) = strftime('%m', T2.charttime) 
AND T1.charttime < T2.charttime
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'arterial catheterization'], ["strftime('%m',t1.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%m', t2.charttime)"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region and thigh' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'arterial catheterization' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'arterial catheterization'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

Among patients who were prescribed with potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns, what are the top four most frequently prescribed drugs at the same time since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T1.drug FROM (
    SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM prescriptions
    WHERE prescriptions.subject_id IN (
        SELECT DISTINCT subject_id
        FROM prescriptions
        WHERE drug = 'potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns'
    ) AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    GROUP BY prescriptions.drug
) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['t1.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(t1.starttime)', '=', 'datetime(t2.starttime)'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------

Among those who were prescribed with lidocaine 0.5%, what are the five most commonly prescribed drugs at the same time since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT prescriptions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'lidocaine 0.5%' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) >= '2100' ) AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) >= '2100' GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.hadm_id', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'lidocaine 0.5%'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'lidocaine 0.5%' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['datetime(t1.starttime)', '=', 'datetime(t2.starttime)'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'lidocaine 0.5%'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with other psoriasis?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other psoriasis' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )

What are the four most frequent drugs that were prescribed to male patients in their 30s during the same month after they were diagnosed with hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia during this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code IN (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', current_time)
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id
        WHERE patients.gender = 'm'
        AND admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39
        AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y', current_time)
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'hyposmolality'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'm'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'm' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4

Since 2100, how many patients were diagnosed with cyst and pseudocyst of pancreas in the same month after being diagnosed with secondary diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, not stated as uncontrolled, or unspecified?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'secondary diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, not stated as uncontrolled, or unspecified'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'cyst and pseudocyst of pancreas'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime, 'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'cyst'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'secondary diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, not stated as uncontrolled, or unspecified'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

TRUTH:

        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'insertion of intercostal catheter for drainage'
    ) 
    AND procedures_icd.charttime BETWEEN (
        SELECT diagnoses_icd.charttime 
        FROM diagnoses_icd

            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'home accidents'

    )

)

What are the four most frequently ordered medications that male patients aged 20s were prescribed within 2 months after they were diagnosed with mitral valve disorders since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'mitral valve disorders'
        )
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29
        AND patients.gender = 'm'
        AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'mitral valve disorders'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'm'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'mitral valve disorders' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'm' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'mitral valve disorders'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'm'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

What are the five commonly ordered medications for patients aged 50s?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T2.drug FROM (
    SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM prescriptions
    JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
    WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
    GROUP BY prescriptions.drug
) AS T2 WHERE T2.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't2.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['t2.c1', '<=', '5']]}

Among patients in their 60 or above , what are the top three prescribed drugs?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T1.drug FROM (
    SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM prescriptions
    JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
    WHERE admissions.age >= 60
    GROUP BY prescriptions.drug
) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.age', '>=', '60'], ['t1.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.age >= 60 ) GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.age', '>=', '60'], ['t1.c1', '<=', '3']]}

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with history of falling?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) as C1 FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'history of falling' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'history of falling'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'history of falling' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'history of falling'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

How many patients underwent single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass during the same month after the diagnosis with arthropathy, unspecified, lower leg, in 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'arthropathy, unspecified, lower leg'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime, 'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'arthropathy, unspecified, lower leg'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'arthropathy, unspecified, lower leg' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'arthropathy, unspecified, lower leg'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

How many patients died after having been diagnosed with facial weakness during the same hospital encounter throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
JOIN diagnoses_icd ON admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id 
JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id 
WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE long_title = 'facial weakness') 
AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', current_time) 
AND patients.dod IS NOT NULL 
AND datetime(patients.dod, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime, T1.hadm_id FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'facial weakness' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE T2.hadm_id = T3.hadm_id

What is the three most frequently given microbiology tests since 1 year ago for patients who were previously diagnosed with cervical spondylosis without myelopathy within the same hospital visit?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.test_name FROM (
    SELECT T2.test_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'cervical spondylosis without myelopathy'
        )
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.test_name, microbiologyevents.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM microbiologyevents
        JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    GROUP BY T2.test_name
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.test_name', 't2.test_name', 't3.test_name'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'cervical spondylosis without myelopathy'], ['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.test_name FROM ( SELECT T2.test_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'cervical spondylosis without myelopathy' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.test_name, microbiologyevents.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.test_name ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.test_name', 't2.test_name', 't3.test_name'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'cervical spondylosis without myelopathy'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

----------
